// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The dominant work is the greedy “best seed” search: each outer cluster is sequential, but all candidate seeds are independent for that iteration. I’m preserving the original strict tie-breaking (largest cluster, then earliest seed; earliest candidate on equal distance) while parallelizing that search with a persistent OpenMP team. I’ll also remove avoidable per-seed allocations and use the already-maintained sorted active-point list to reduce overhead.

+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                    best_cluster_members.clear();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster_marks, generation, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }

+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+

+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster_marks, generation, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
                     const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& in_cluster_marks,
                     const std::uint32_t generation,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // unclustered_indices remains sorted, so this has the same candidate order
    // as the original point_count-wide loop without testing clustered points.
    for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
        // Skip points already added to this candidate cluster.
        if (in_cluster_marks[candidate] == generation) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);

            // max_dist can only increase. Once this candidate cannot satisfy
            // either strict comparison, the remaining distances are irrelevant.
            if (max_dist >= threshold || max_dist >= min_diameter) break;
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              std::vector<std::uint32_t>& in_cluster_marks,
                              std::uint32_t& generation,
                              std::vector<int>& members,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    ++generation;
    if (generation == 0) {
        // Keep the stamp scheme correct even after a uint32_t wraparound.
        std::fill(in_cluster_marks.begin(), in_cluster_marks.end(), 0);
        ++generation;
    }
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster_marks[seed_point] = generation;
    members.clear();
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size())) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, unclustered_indices,
                                             in_cluster_marks, generation, points,
                                             threshold);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster_marks[closest] = generation;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());

    int active_count = 0;
    int max_cardinality = -1;
    int best_seed = -1;
    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    bool done = false;
    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;

#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_cardinality, best_seed, best_position, best_cluster_members, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
    {
        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
        std::vector<std::uint32_t> in_cluster_marks(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
        std::vector<int> members;
        std::uint32_t generation = 0;

        while (true) {
#pragma omp single
            {
                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
                    done = true;
                } else {
                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
                    max_cardinality = -1;
                    best_seed = -1;
                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
                    best_cluster_members.clear();
                }
            }

            if (done) break;

            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
                if (clustered[seed]) continue;

                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
                    in_cluster_marks, generation, members);

                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
                    local_best_position = i;
                }
            }

            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;

#pragma omp barrier
#pragma omp single
            {
                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
                                        ? -1
                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
                    }
                }

                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
                    Cluster cluster;
                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
                    generateCandidateCluster(

--- CMake ---

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<std::uint32_t> in_cluster_marks(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        std::uint32_t generation = 0;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

             }

-

-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster_marks, generation, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }

-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster_marks, generation, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];

codex
The OpenMP build now compiles cleanly. A 1-thread run reproduces the baseline membership hash exactly, and validation passes; with four threads the same result is produced while the measured clustering phase drops from about 1.18 s to 0.30 s on this machine. I’m running broader tie/order and threshold checks next, then I’ll inspect the final diff for portability and race issues.
exec
/bin/bash -lc 'set -e
for n in 100 300 1000; do
  for t in 0.1 1.0 2.0 10.0; do
    baseline=$(/tmp/qtclustering-baseline -n "$n" -t "$t" -r | awk '"'/Clusters found:|Hash:/{print}')
    omp1="'$(OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 /tmp/qtclustering-omp-build/qtclustering -n "$n" -t "$t" -r | awk '"'/Clusters found:|Hash:/{print}')
    omp4="'$(OMP_NUM_THREADS=4 /tmp/qtclustering-omp-build/qtclustering -n "$n" -t "$t" -r | awk '"'/Clusters found:|Hash:/{print}')
    if [ \""'$baseline" != "$omp1" ] || [ "$baseline" != "$omp4" ]; then
      printf '"'MISMATCH n=%s t=%s\\nBASELINE\\n%s\\nOMP1\\n%s\\nOMP4\\n%s\\n' \""'$n" "$t" "$baseline" "$omp1" "$omp4"
      exit 1
    fi
    printf '"'match n=%s t=%s %s\\n' \""'$n" "$t" "$(printf '"'%s' \""'$omp4" | tr '"'\\n' ' ')\"
  done
done" in /home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_omp_r3

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<std::uint32_t> in_cluster_marks(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        std::uint32_t generation = 0;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster_marks, generation, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster_marks, generation, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<std::uint32_t> in_cluster_marks(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        std::uint32_t generation = 0;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());

+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster_marks, generation, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }

-            );

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster_marks, generation, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

                    // No more clusters can be formed.
                    done = true;
                }
            }

            if (done) break;

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }

+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster_marks, generation, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }
 

codex
The broader checks confirm exact cluster counts and hashes for several point counts, thresholds, and thread counts where completed. I’m making one small memory-scalability refinement before the final pass: per-thread membership scratch will use byte marks cleared over the generated cluster, avoiding an O(threads × points × 4-byte) stamp array while retaining fast indexed checks.

+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();

+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());

+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;

+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;

+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+

+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
+            }
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+

+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }

     }

+
     return clusters;

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
             }
-        }
-

-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;

-            // Mark all members as clustered

-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
+            }
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
+            }
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

             }
-        }
-

-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;

-            // Mark all members as clustered

-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
+            }
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    int max_cardinality = -1;

+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {

+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
