+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||

+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }

+#pragma omp single

+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {

+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())

+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];

+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.

+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+

+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),

+    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;

         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
     
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-

-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+

+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());

+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

+            if (done) break;

+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.

+

+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.

+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {

+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];

+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {

+                    }

+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),

codex
The stale jobs are cleared. The implementation’s synchronization is sound: candidate evaluations finish at an explicit barrier, one thread performs the deterministic reduction and greedy state update, and the next iteration begins only after the update’s barrier. CMake also shows `-fopenmp` on compilation and links the OpenMP runtime through `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)

project(qtclustering)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)

add_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)

target_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

target_link_libraries(qtclustering PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());

    int active_count = 0;
    int max_cardinality = -1;
    int best_seed = -1;
    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    bool done = false;

#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
    {
        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
        std::vector<int> members;

        while (true) {
#pragma omp single
            {
                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
                    done = true;
                } else {
                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
                    max_cardinality = -1;
                    best_seed = -1;
                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
                }
            }

            if (done) break;

            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();

            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
                if (clustered[seed]) continue;

                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
                    in_cluster, members);

                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
                    local_best_position = i;
                }
            }

            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;

#pragma omp barrier
#pragma omp single
            {
                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
                                        ? -1
                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
                    }
                }

                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
                    Cluster cluster;
                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
                    generateCandidateCluster(
                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));

                    // Mark all members as clustered.
                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
                        clustered[member] = 1;
                    }

                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
                    unclustered_indices.erase(
                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
                        unclustered_indices.end());
                } else {
                    // No more clusters can be formed.
                    done = true;
                }
            }

            if (done) break;

diff --git a/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt b/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt

-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;

-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {

-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all greedy iterations. The iterations
+    // themselves are ordered, but evaluating all possible seeds in an iteration
+    // is independent and is the dominant cost of QT clustering.
+    omp_set_dynamic(0);
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization.

- Parallelized independent seed evaluations with a persistent OpenMP team and guided scheduling.
- Preserved deterministic tie-breaking and cluster semantics.
- Reduced allocation and distance-computation overhead.
- Updated [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_omp_r3/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp) and [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_omp_r3/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt).
- OpenMP is required and linked unconditionally; executable remains `qtclustering`.

Verified clean build, validation success, and matching baseline hash `dbef3881b0a560cd`. For 1000 points, clustering improved from ~7.0 s baseline to ~0.30 s with four threads.

+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    const int parallel_threads = std::min(max_threads, N);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_cardinality(max_threads, -1);
+    std::vector<int> thread_best_position(max_threads, std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    int active_count = 0;
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    bool done = false;
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(parallel_threads) shared(active_count, best_seed, best_position, clustered, done, max_cardinality, points, threshold, thread_best_cardinality, thread_best_position, unclustered_indices, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
+        std::vector<int> members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                } else {
+                    active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                    max_cardinality = -1;
+                    best_seed = -1;
+                    best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (done) break;
+
+            int local_best_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_position = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+
+            // Guided scheduling balances seeds with different candidate-cluster
+            // sizes while retaining low scheduling overhead for large inputs.
+#pragma omp for schedule(guided) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
+                if (clustered[seed]) continue;
+
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                    in_cluster, members);
+
+                // Explicitly compare positions so reduction order cannot alter
+                // the original first-seed-wins tie behavior.
+                if (cardinality > local_best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best_cardinality && i < local_best_position)) {
+                    local_best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_position = i;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            thread_best_cardinality[thread_id] = local_best_cardinality;
+            thread_best_position[thread_id] = local_best_position;
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (int t = 0; t < max_threads; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best_cardinality[t] > max_cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best_cardinality[t] == max_cardinality &&
+                         thread_best_position[t] < best_position)) {
+                        max_cardinality = thread_best_cardinality[t];
+                        best_position = thread_best_position[t];
+                        best_seed = (best_position == std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
+                                        ? -1
+                                        : unclustered_indices[static_cast<size_t>(best_position)];
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // If we found a cluster, add it. Rebuilding only the winning
+                // cluster avoids copying candidate vectors for every seed.
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    generateCandidateCluster(
+                        best_seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold,
+                        in_cluster, members, &cluster.members);
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    // Mark all members as clustered.
+                    for (const int member : clusters.back().members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    // Remove clustered points from the sorted active list.
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                      [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    // No more clusters can be formed.
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+
+            if (done) break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }
